# Oil engine glossary: every Russian name in the code

Generated by `python3 docs/english/ru2en.py --glossary docs/english/GLOSSARY.md`. English is a word-by-word reading aid, built from the engine's own handbook dictionary (`движок/кисть/словарь_справки.py`) plus `docs/english/words_extra.py`. A `~` marks a word that is not in the glossary and was transliterated. To improve a translation, add the word to `words_extra.py` and regenerate.

3673 names, 40 with at least one transliterated word.
